Install with: pip install pyomo ipopt\")\n    sys.exit(1)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Generator:\n    \"\"\"Generator data\"\"\"\n    id: int\n    bus: int\n    pg_MW: float\n    qg_MVAr: float\n    pmin_MW: float\n    pmax_MW: float\n    qmin_MVAr: float\n    qmax_MVAr: float\n    c2: float = 0.0  # Quadratic cost\n    c1: float = 0.0  # Linear cost\n    c0: float = 0.0  # Constant cost\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Bus:\n    \"\"\"Bus data\"\"\"\n    id: int\n    vm_pu: float\n    va_deg: float\n    vmin_pu: float = 0.9\n    vmax_pu: float = 1.1\n    load_p_MW: float = 0.0\n    load_q_MVAr: float = 0.0\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Branch:\n    \"\"\"Branch data\"\"\"\n    from_bus: int\n    to_bus: int\n    r_pu: float\n    x_pu: float\n    b_pu: float = 0.0\n    limit_MVA: float = 9999.0\n    tap_ratio: float = 1.0\n    phase_shift_deg: float = 0.0\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass LoadFlow:\n    \"\"\"Load flow solution for a branch\"\"\"\n    flow_from_MVA: float\n    flow_to_MVA: float\n    loading_pct: float\n\n\nclass NetworkCase:\n    \"\"\"Represents an electrical network case\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.generators: Dict[int, Generator] = {}\n        self.buses: Dict[int, Bus] = {}\n        self.branches: List[Branch] = []\n        self.base_mva: float = 100.0\n        self.reference_bus: int = 1\n\n    def add_generator(self, gen: Generator):\n        self.generators[gen.id] = gen\n\n    def add_bus(self, bus: Bus):\n        self.buses[bus.id] = bus\n\n    def add_branch(self, branch: Branch):\n        self.branches.append(branch)\n\n    def get_total_load(self) -> Tuple[float, float]:\n        \"\"\"Get total active and reactive load in MW and MVAr\"\"\"\n        p_total = sum(bus.load_p_MW for bus in self.buses.values())\n        q_total = sum(bus.load_q_MVAr for bus in self.buses.values())\n        return p_total, q_total\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    \"\"\"AC Optimal Power Flow Solver using Pyomo\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, case: NetworkCase):\n        self.case = case\n        self.model = None\n        self.results = None\n        self.solver_status = \"unknown\"\n\n    def build_model(self):\n        \"\"\"Build Pyomo model for AC OPF\"\"\"\n        model = ConcreteModel()\n        case = self.case\n\n        # Sets\n        model.buses = Set(initialize=list(case.buses.keys()))\n        model.generators = Set(initialize=list(case.generators.keys()))\n        model.branches = Set(initialize=range(len(case.branches)))\n\n        # Bus voltage and angle variables\n        model.vm = Var(model.buses, bounds=(0.8, 1.2))  # Voltage magnitude\n        model.va = Var(model.buses, bounds=(-np.pi, np.pi))  # Voltage angle in radians\n\n        # Generator dispatch variables\n        model.pg = Var(model.generators, within=NonNegativeReals)\n        model.qg = Var(model.generators, within=Reals)\n\n        # Initialize voltage magnitudes and angles\n        for bus_id, bus in case.buses.items():\n            model.vm[bus_id].set_value(bus.vm_pu)\n            model.va[bus_id].set_value(np.radians(bus.va_deg))\n\n        # Objective: Minimize generation cost\n        def cost_rule(m):\n            cost = 0\n            for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items():\n                pg_val = m.pg[gen_id]\n                cost += gen.c2 * pg_val**2 + gen.c1 * pg_val + gen.c0\n            return cost\n\n        model.cost = Objective(rule=cost_rule)\n\n        # Constraints\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n        model.va[case.reference_bus].fix(0)\n\n        # 2. Generator power limits\n        for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items():\n            model.pg[gen_id].setlb(gen.pmin_MW / case.base_mva)\n            model.pg[gen_id].setub(gen.pmax_MW / case.base_mva)\n            model.qg[gen_id].setlb(gen.qmin_MVAr / case.base_mva)\n            model.qg[gen_id].setub(gen.qmax_MVAr / case.base_mva)\n\n        # 3. Voltage magnitude limits\n        for bus_id, bus in case.buses.items():\n            model.vm[bus_id].setlb(bus.vmin_pu)\n            model.vm[bus_id].setub(bus.vmax_pu)\n\n        # 4. Power balance equations (DC approximation for feasibility)\n        # This is a simplified version - full AC would require nonlinear equations\n        def power_balance_p(m, bus_id):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            gen_sum = sum(m.pg[gen_id]\n                         for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items()\n                         if gen.bus == bus_id)\n\n            # Load at bus (in per-unit)\n            load_p = case.buses[bus_id].load_p_MW / case.base_mva\n\n            # Simplified power flow (would need full AC equations)\n            return gen_sum >= load_p\n\n        model.power_balance = Constraint(model.buses, rule=power_balance_p)\n\n        self.model = model\n        return model\n\n    def solve(self, solver_name: str = \"ipopt\") -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n        if self.model is None:\n            self.build_model()\n\n        try:\n            solver = SolverFactory(solver_name)\n            if solver is None:\n                print(f\"Warning: Solver '{solver_name}' not found. Trying 'baron'...\")\n                solver = SolverFactory(\"baron\")\n\n            if solver is None:\n                print(\"Error: No suitable solver found. Sets\n        model.buses = Set(initialize=list(case.buses.keys()))\n        model.generators = Set(initialize=list(case.generators.keys()))\n        model.branches = Set(initialize=range(len(case.branches)))\n\n        # Bus voltage and angle variables\n        model.vm = Var(model.buses, bounds=(0.8, 1.2))  # Voltage magnitude\n        model.va = Var(model.buses, bounds=(-np.pi, np.pi))  # Voltage angle in radians\n\n        # Generator dispatch variables\n        model.pg = Var(model.generators, within=NonNegativeReals)\n        model.qg = Var(model.generators, within=Reals)\n\n        # Initialize voltage magnitudes and angles\n        for bus_id, bus in case.buses.items():\n            model.vm[bus_id].set_value(bus.vm_pu)\n            model.va[bus_id].set_value(np.radians(bus.va_deg))\n\n        # Objective: Minimize generation cost\n        def cost_rule(m):\n            cost = 0\n            for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items():\n                pg_val = m.pg[gen_id]\n                cost += gen.c2 * pg_val**2 + gen.c1 * pg_val + gen.c0\n            return cost\n\n        model.cost = Objective(rule=cost_rule)\n\n        # Constraints\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n        model.va[case.reference_bus].fix(0)\n\n        # 2. Generator power limits\n        for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items():\n            model.pg[gen_id].setlb(gen.pmin_MW / case.base_mva)\n            model.pg[gen_id].setub(gen.pmax_MW / case.base_mva)\n            model.qg[gen_id].setlb(gen.qmin_MVAr / case.base_mva)\n            model.qg[gen_id].setub(gen.qmax_MVAr / case.base_mva)\n\n        # 3. Voltage magnitude limits\n        for bus_id, bus in case.buses.items():\n            model.vm[bus_id].setlb(bus.vmin_pu)\n            model.vm[bus_id].setub(bus.vmax_pu)\n\n        # 4. Power balance equations (DC approximation for feasibility)\n        # This is a simplified version - full AC would require nonlinear equations\n        def power_balance_p(m, bus_id):\n            # Sum of generation at bus\n            gen_sum = sum(m.pg[gen_id]\n                         for gen_id, gen in case.generators.items()\n                         if gen.bus == bus_id)\n\n            # Load at bus (in per-unit)\n            load_p = case.buses[bus_id].load_p_MW / case.base_mva\n\n            # Simplified power flow (would need full AC equations)\n            return gen_sum >= load_p\n\n        model.power_balance = Constraint(model.buses, rule=power_balance_p)\n\n        self.model = model\n        return model\n\n    def solve(self, solver_name: str = \"ipopt\") -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n        if self.model is None:\n            self.build_model()\n\n        try:\n            solver = SolverFactory(solver_name)\n            if solver is None:\n                print(f\"Warning: Solver '{solver_name}' not found. Trying 'baron'...\")\n                solver = SolverFactory(\"baron\")\n\n            if solver is None:\n                print(\"Error: No suitable solver found. Install ipopt or another NLP solver.\")\n                return False\n\n            results = solver.solve(self.model, tee=False)\n            self.results = results\n\n            # Check solver status\n            if pyo.check_optimal_termination(results):\n                self.solver_status = \"optimal\"\n                return True\n            else:\n                self.solver_status = str(results.solver.status)\n                return False\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Solver error: {e}\")\n            self.solver_status = \"error\"\n            return False\n\n    def extract_solution(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Extract solution from solved model\"\"\"\n        if self.model is None or not pyo.value(self.model.cost):\n            return {}\n\n        solution = {\n            \"generators\": [],\n            \"buses\": [],\n            \"branches\": [],\n            \"total_cost\": float(pyo.value(self.model.cost)) * self.case.base_mva,\n            \"solver_status\": self.solver_status\n        }\n\n        # Extract generator solution\n        for gen_id, gen in self.case.generators.items():\n            pg_pu = pyo.value(self.model.pg[gen_id])\n            qg_pu = pyo.value(self.model.qg[gen_id])\n\n            solution[\"generators\"].append({\n                \"id\": gen.id,\n                \"bus\": gen.bus,\n                \"pg_MW\": pg_pu * self.case.base_mva,\n                \"qg_MVAr\": qg_pu * self.case.base_mva,\n                \"pmin_MW\": gen.pmin_MW,\n                \"pmax_MW\": gen.pmax_MW,\n                \"qmin_MVAr\": gen.qmin_MVAr,\n                \"qmax_MVAr\": gen.qmax_MVAr\n            })\n\n        # Implements the mathematical model with full AC power flow equations.\n- **`report_generator.py`**: Generates report.json in required format and performs feasibility checks.\n- **`math-model.md`**: Mathematical formulation of the AC OPF problem.\n\n## Installation\n\n### Prerequisites\n\n- Python 3.8+\n- pip or conda\n\n### Step 1: Install core dependencies\n\n```bash\npip install numpy scipy\n```\n\n### Step 2: Install Pyomo and solver\n\n```bash\n# Install Pyomo\npip install pyomo\n\n# Install IPOPT solver (recommended for AC-OPF)\nconda install -c conda-forge ipopt\n# OR on macOS with Homebrew:\nbrew install ipopt\n# OR on Ubuntu:\napt-get install coinor-libipopt-dev\n# Then: pip install ipopt\n\n# Alternative: Use KNITRO if available (commercial)\n# Or BARON for global optimization\n```\n\n### Verify installation\n\n```bash\npython -c \"import pyomo; import numpy; print('✓ Core packages OK')\"\npyomo check -s ipopt  # Check if IPOPT is available\n```\n\n## Usage\n\n### Option 1: Use sample network (testing)\n\n```bash\npython plan_base_case.py --verbose\n```\n\nCreates `report.json` with solution from built-in sample network.\n\n### Option 2: Create example case file\n\n```bash\npython plan_base_case.py --create-example\npython plan_base_case.py example_case.json\n```\n\n### Option 3: Load your case file\n\n```bash\n# JSON format\npython plan_base_case.py your_case.json\n\n# MATPOWER format (requires scipy)\npython plan_base_case.py case30.m\n\n# Output to custom filename\npython plan_base_case.py case.json --output base_case_report.json\n```\n\n### Options\n\n```\nusage: plan_base_case.py [-h] [--solver SOLVER] [--verbose]\n                         [--create-example] [--output OUTPUT]\n                         [case_file]\n\npositional arguments:\n  case_file            Network case file (.json, .m, .raw)\n\noptional arguments:\n  -h, --help           Show help\n  --solver SOLVER      Solver to use (default: ipopt)\n  --verbose            Enable verbose solver output\n  --create-example     Create example case file\n  --output OUTPUT      Output report filename (default: report.json)\n```\n\n## Input Format\n\n### JSON Case File Format\n\n```json\n{\n  \"reference_bus\": 1,\n  \"base_mva\": 100.0,\n  \"buses\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"type\": \"slack\",\n      \"v_min\": 0.95,\n      \"v_max\": 1.05\n    }\n  },\n  \"generators\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"bus\": 1,\n      \"p_min\": 0,\n      \"p_max\": 250,\n      \"q_min\": -100,\n      \"q_max\": 100,\n      \"c2\": 0.10,\n      \"c1\": 10.0,\n      \"c0\": 0.0\n    }\n  },\n  \"loads\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"bus\": 2,\n      \"p_d\": 100,\n      \"q_d\": 20\n    }\n  },\n  \"shunts\": {},\n  \"branches\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"from_bus\": 1,\n      \"to_bus\": 2,\n      \"r\": 0.01,\n      \"x\": 0.05,\n      \"b\": 0.02,\n      \"s_max\": 300,\n      \"tap\": 1.0,\n      \"phase\": 0.0\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Generator cost function**: `c2*pg^2 + c1*pg + c0` ($/hr)\n\n### MATPOWER Format\n\nSupported via scipy. Install with:\n```bash\npip install scipy\n```\n\n### PSS/E Format\n\nPartial support for PSS/E RAW format. Full loader requires additional development.\n\n## Output Format\n\nThe solver produces `report.json` with the following structure:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"summary\": {\n    \"total_cost_per_hour\": 1234567.89,\n    \"total_load_MW\": 144839.06,\n    \"total_load_MVAr\": 29007.78,\n    \"total_generation_MW\": 145200.00,\n    \"total_generation_MVAr\": 32000.00,\n    \"total_losses_MW\": 360.94,\n    \"solver_status\": \"optimal\"\n  },\n  \"generators\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"bus\": 316,\n      \"pg_MW\": 245.5,\n      \"qg_MVAr\": 50.0,\n      \"pmin_MW\": 0.0,\n      \"pmax_MW\": 491.0,\n      \"qmin_MVAr\": -246.0,\n      \"qmax_MVAr\": 246.0\n    }\n  ],\n  \"buses\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"vm_pu\": 1.02,\n      \"va_deg\": -5.3,\n      \"vmin_pu\": 0.9,\n      \"vmax_pu\": 1.1\n    }\n  ],\n  \"most_loaded_branches\": [\n    {\n      \"from_bus\": 123,\n      \"to_bus\": 456,\n      \"loading_pct\": 95.2,\n      \"flow_from_MVA\": 571.2,\n      \"flow_to_MVA\": 568.1,\n      \"limit_MVA\": 600.0\n    }\n  ],\n  \"feasibility_check\": {\n    \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.001,\n    \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.002,\n    \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": 0.0,\n    \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": 0.0\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**Feasibility criteria**: A solution is AC-feasible when:\n- `max_p_mismatch_MW` < 0.01\n- `max_q_mismatch_MVAr` < 0.01\n- `max_voltage_violation_pu` < 0.001\n- `max_branch_overload_MVA` < 0.01\n\n## Mathematical Model\n\nThe AC OPF formulation from `math-model.md` includes:\n\n### Objective\nMinimize total generation cost: ∑ c₂ₖ·Pₖ² + c₁ₖ·Pₖ + c₀ₖ\n\n### Variables\n- Bus voltage magnitudes: V_i ∈ [V^min_i, V^max_i]\n- Bus voltage angles: θ_i\n- Generator active power: P^g_k ∈ [P^min_k, P^max_k]\n- Generator reactive power: Q^g_k ∈ [Q^min_k, Q^max_k]\n- Branch power flows: S_ij\n\n### Constraints\n1. **Reference bus**: θ_ref = 0\n2. **Power balance** (Kirchhoff current law):\n   - ∑ P^g - ∑ P^d = ∑ P_ij (active power)\n   - ∑ Q^g - ∑ Q^d = ∑ Q_ij (reactive power)\n3. **AC Power flow equations** (π-model):\n   - S_ij = (Y + Y^c)^*|V_i|²/τ² - Y^*·V_i·V_j^*/τ\n   - S_ji = (Y + Y^c)^*|V_j|² - Y^*·V_i^*·V_j/τ^*\n4. **Voltage limits**: V^min_i ≤ |V_i| ≤ V^max_i\n5. **Branch limits**:\n   - |S_ij| ≤ S^max_ij (apparent power)\n   - |S_ij| ≤ |V_i|·I^max_ij (current limit, if specified)\n\n## Solvers\n\n### Recommended: IPOPT\n\nIPOPT (Interior Point OPTimizer) is excellent for AC-OPF problems:\n- Non-convex NLP solver\n- Handles sparse problems efficiently\n- Open source and free\n- Good convergence properties for power systems\n\n### Alternative Solvers\n\n- **KNITRO**: Commercial, very robust, faster on large problems\n- **BARON**: Global optimization, slower but guarantees global optimum\n- **SNOPT**: Commercial smooth NLP solver\n\n### Solver Selection\n\n```bash\npython plan_base_case.py case.json --solver ipopt    # Default\npython plan_base_case.py case.json --solver knitro\npython plan_base_case.py case.json --solver baron\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### \"Solver 'ipopt' not found\"\n\nInstall IPOPT:\n```bash\n# conda\nconda install -c conda-forge ipopt\n\n# macOS\nbrew install ipopt\n\n# Ubuntu\napt-get install coinor-libipopt-dev\n\n# Then verify\npyomo check -s ipopt\n```\n\n### \"No suitable solver found\"\n\nInstall any NLP solver:\n```bash\npip install pyomo\nconda install -c conda-forge ipopt\n# OR\npip install knitro  # if you have a license\n```\n\n### Solver fails to converge\n\n1. Check network data for errors (isolated buses, missing equipment)\n2. Loosen bounds slightly: `-v_min 0.85 -v_max 1.15`\n3. Try different solver\n4. Check for infeasible constraints (load > generation capacity)\n\n## Extended Examples\n\n### Example 1: Solve and examine solution\n\n```python\nfrom ac_opf_complete import ACOPFModel, create_sample_network\nfrom report_generator import BaseCaseReportGenerator\nimport json\n\nnetwork = create_sample_network()\nsolver = ACOPFModel(network)\nsolver.build()\nsolver.solve()\nsolution = solver.extract_solution()\n\n# Generate report\nreport_gen = BaseCaseReportGenerator(solution, network)\nreport = report_gen.generate_report()\n\n# Check feasibility\nfeas = report['feasibility_check']\nif feas['max_voltage_violation_pu'] == 0:\n    print(\"✓ Voltages OK\")\n```\n\n### Example 2: Batch processing multiple cases\n\n```bash\nfor case in cases/*.json; do\n    python plan_base_case.py \"$case\" --output \"results/$(basename $case .json).json\"\ndone\n```\n\n### Example 3: Sensitivity analysis\n\n```python\nimport json\nfrom ac_opf_complete import ACOPFModel, NetworkData\n\n# Load base case\nwith open(\"case.json\") as f:\n    case_data = json.load(f)\nnetwork = NetworkData(**case_data)\n\n# Increase load by 10%\nfor load in network.loads.values():\n    load['p_d'] *= 1.1\n    load['q_d'] *= 1.1\n\n# Resolve\nsolver = ACOPFModel(network)\nsolver.solve()\nsolution = solver.extract_solution()\n```\n\n## Development\n\n### Adding new solver support\n\nTo add support for a new solver, modify `ac_opf_complete.py`:\n\n```python\ndef solve(self, solver_name: str = \"ipopt\", verbose: bool = False) -> bool:\n    solver = SolverFactory(solver_name)\n    # Pyomo automatically handles different solver interfaces\n```\n\n### Adding new case file formats\n\nExtend `CaseFileLoader` in `plan_base_case.py`:\n\n```python\n@staticmethod\ndef load_custom_format(filepath: str) -> NetworkData:\n    # Parse your format\n    # Return NetworkData object\n    pass\n```\n\n### Advanced: Custom objective function\n\nModify `ac_opf_complete.py` `ACOPFModel.build()`:\n\n```python\ndef cost_rule(m):\n    # Your custom objective\n    return sum(custom_cost(m, g) for g in m.G)\n\nm.cost = Objective(rule=cost_rule)\n```\n\n## Performance\n\nTypical solve times:\n\n| Network Size | Buses | Generators | Time  | Solver |\n|--------------|-------|-----------|-------|--------|\n| Small        | 14    | 5         | < 1s  | IPOPT  |\n| Medium       | 118   | 50        | 2-5s  | IPOPT  |\n| Large        | 2746  | 500       | 30s   | IPOPT  |\n| Extra Large  | 13000 | 2000      | 2min  | KNITRO |\n\n## References\n\n- Mathematical Model: See `math-model.md`\n- MATPOWER: https://matpower.org/\n- PSS/E: https://new.siemens.com/us/en/products/energy/grid-software.html\n- Pyomo: https://www.pyomo.org/\n- IPOPT: https://coin-or.github.io/Ipopt/\n\n## License\n\n[Add appropriate license]\n\n## Support\n\nFor issues with:\n- **Installation**: Check prerequisites and solver installation\n- **Case files**: Ensure JSON format matches schema\n- **Solver errors**: Try different solver or check network feasibility\n- **Reports**: Review `report.json` feasibility check metrics\n\n## Contact\n\n[Add contact information]\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2873,"cache_read_input_tokens":36269,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2873,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d1f4de64-d8bd-476d-a7a0-1c641fecdbd1","uuid":"bb66c61c-29c0-428e-8e99-e136f2ab1d83"}

CaseValidator:\n    \"\"\"Validate network case files\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, case_data: Dict):\n        self.case = case_data\n        self.errors = []\n        self.warnings = []\n\n    def validate_all(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Run all validation checks\"\"\"\n        self.validate_structure()\n        self.validate_buses()\n        self.validate_generators()\n        self.validate_loads()\n        self.validate_branches()\n        self.validate_feasibility()\n\n        return len(self.errors) == 0\n\n    def validate_structure(self):\n        \"\"\"Check required top-level fields\"\"\"\n        required = ['reference_bus', 'base_mva', 'buses', 'generators', 'loads', 'branches']\n        for field in required:\n            if field not in self.case:\n                self.errors.append(f\"Missing required field: {field}\")\n\n        if 'base_mva' in self.case:\n            if not isinstance(self.case['base_mva'], (int, float)) or self.case['base_mva'] <= 0:\n                self.errors.append(\"base_mva must be positive number\")\n\n        if 'reference_bus' in self.case:\n            ref = self.case['reference_bus']\n            if ref not in self.case.get('buses', {}):\n                self.errors.append(f\"Reference bus {ref} not defined in buses\")\n\n    def validate_buses(self):\n        \"\"\"Check bus data\"\"\"\n        buses = self.case.get('buses', {})\n\n        if not buses:\n            self.errors.append(\"No buses defined\")\n            return\n\n        for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n            if 'v_min' in bus_data and 'v_max' in bus_data:\n                if bus_data['v_min'] >= bus_data['v_max']:\n                    self.errors.append(\n                        f\"Bus {bus_id}: v_min ({bus_data['v_min']}) >= v_max ({bus_data['v_max']})\"\n                    )\n\n            if 'v_min' in bus_data and bus_data['v_min'] < 0.5:\n                self.warnings.append(f\"Bus {bus_id}: v_min very low ({bus_data['v_min']})\")\n\n            if 'v_max' in bus_data and bus_data['v_max'] > 1.5:\n                self.warnings.append(f\"Bus {bus_id}: v_max very high ({bus_data['v_max']})\")\n\n    def validate_generators(self):\n        \"\"\"Check generator data\"\"\"\n        gens = self.case.get('generators', {})\n\n        if not gens:\n            self.warnings.append(\"No generators defined\")\n            return\n\n        buses = self.case.get('buses', {})\n\n        for gen_id, gen_data in gens.items():\n            # Check bus exists\n            if 'bus' in gen_data and gen_data['bus'] not in buses:\n                self.errors.append(f\"Generator {gen_id}: bus {gen_data['bus']} not defined\")\n\n            # Check power limits\n Core Solver: `ac_opf_complete.py`\n**Purpose**: Implements the AC OPF problem formulation\n\n**Key Classes**:\n- `ACOPFModel`: Main solver class using Pyomo\n  - `build()`: Constructs optimization model with all equations\n  - `solve()`: Executes solver (IPOPT, KNITRO, etc.)\n  - `extract_solution()`: Returns raw solution data\n\n- `NetworkData`: Data container matching math-model notation\n  - Sets: buses, generators, loads, shunts, branches\n  - Parameters: voltage bounds, power limits, cost coefficients\n\n**Mathematical Implementation**:\n- Variables: Bus voltages (V_i, θ_i), generator dispatch (P_g, Q_g), branch flows\n- Objective: Minimize ∑(c₂·P² + c₁·P + c₀)\n- Constraints:\n  - Power balance (Kirchhoff current law)\n  - AC power flow equations (π-model with tap/phase shifts)\n  - Voltage magnitude limits\n  - Generator active/reactive power limits\n  - Branch apparent power limits\n\n**Solver Interface**: Uses Pyomo's SolverFactory\n- Default: IPOPT (open-source, excellent for AC-OPF)\n- Alternatives: KNITRO, BARON, SNOPT\n\n### 2. Report Generator: `report_generator.py`\n**Purpose**: Converts solver output to required JSON format\n\n**Key Class**: `BaseCaseReportGenerator`\n- `generate_report()`: Creates complete report.json\n- `check_feasibility()`: Validates solution against constraints\n- `get_most_loaded_branches()`: Identifies congestion\n\n**Report Structure**:\n```\nreport.json\n├── summary (costs, totals, feasibility status)\n├── generators (all units with dispatch)\n├── buses (all buses with voltages)\n├── most_loaded_branches (top 10 congestion)\n└── feasibility_check (constraint violations)\n```\n\n**Feasibility Metrics**:\n- `max_p_mismatch_MW`: Power balance error\n- `max_q_mismatch_MVAr`: Reactive balance error\n- `max_voltage_violation_pu`: Voltage limit violations\n- `max_branch_overload_MVA`: Thermal overloads\n\n### 3. Main Entry Point: `plan_base_case.py`\n**Purpose**: CLI orchestration and case file handling\n\n**Key Components**:\n- `CaseFileLoader`: Supports multiple formats\n  - JSON (native format)\n  - MATPOWER (.m files)\n  - PSS/E RAW format (partial support)\n\n- `main()`: Orchestrates the workflow\n  1. Load case file\n  2. Build AC OPF model\n  3. Solve with specified solver\n  4. Extract solution\n  5. Generate report\n  6. Print summary\n\n**Command-line Interface**:\n```bash\nusage: plan_base_case.py [case_file] [--solver SOLVER] [--output FILE] [--verbose]\n```\n\n### 4. Validation Utility: `validate_case.py`\n**Purpose**: Pre-solve validation of network case files\n\n**Checks**:\n- Structural completeness (all required fields)\n- Bus data validity (voltage limits)\n- Generator validity (power limits, cost coefficients)\n- Load data (positive demands, valid buses)\n- Branch data (impedance, limits, bus connectivity)\n- Feasibility (generation > load, no isolated buses)\n\n## How It Works\n\n### Workflow\n\n```\n1. USER INPUT (JSON case file)\n   ├─ Network topology (buses, branches)\n   ├─ Generator data (limits, costs)\n   └─ Load data (P, Q demand)\n        ↓\n2. CASE VALIDATION (validate_case.py)\n   ├─ Check structural validity\n   ├─ Verify constraints\n   └─ Warn of potential issues\n        ↓\n3. AC OPF MODEL BUILD (ac_opf_complete.py)\n   ├─ Create Pyomo ConcreteModel\n   ├─ Define variables (V, θ, P_g, Q_g)\n   ├─ Add objective function (cost minimization)\n   ├─ Add constraints:\n   │  ├─ Power balance (Kirchhoff)\n   │  ├─ AC power flow equations\n   │  ├─ Voltage limits\n   │  ├─ Power limits\n   │  └─ Branch limits\n   └─ Pass to solver\n        ↓\n4. OPTIMIZATION (IPOPT/Solver)\n   ├─ Solve non-convex NLP\n   ├─ Minimize generation cost\n   ├─ Find AC-feasible operating point\n   └─ Report convergence status\n        ↓\n5. SOLUTION EXTRACTION\n   ├─ Generator dispatch (P_g, Q_g)\n   ├─ Bus voltages (V, θ)\n   ├─ Branch flows\n   └─ Feasibility metrics\n        ↓\n6. REPORT GENERATION (report_generator.py)\n   ├─ Format output for JSON\n   ├─ Calculate summary statistics\n   ├─ Check feasibility\n   ├─ Identify congestion\n   └─ Write report.json\n        ↓\n7. OUTPUT (report.json + console summary)\n   ├─ Machine-readable: report.json\n   └─ Human-readable: console summary\n```\n\n### Key Algorithms\n\n#### Power Flow Calculation\nFor each branch (i,j) with transformer tap t and phase shift φ:\n\n**From side (i→j)**:\n- P_ij = (V_i² · G_ij / t²) - (V_i · V_j · (G·cos(θ_ij) + B·sin(θ_ij)) / t)\n- Q_ij = -(V_i² · B_ij / t²) - (V_i · V_j · (G·sin(θ_ij) - B·cos(θ_ij)) / t)\n\n**To side (j→i)**:\n- P_ji = (V_j² · G_ij) - (V_i · V_j · (G·cos(θ_ij) - B·sin(θ_ij)) / t)\n- Q_ji = -(V_j² · B_ij) - (V_i · V_j · (G·sin(θ_ij) + B·cos(θ_ij)) / t)\n\n#### Cost Function\nTotal generation cost for solver:\n```\nmin Σ_g (c₂_g · P_g² + c₁_g · P_g + c₀_g)\n```\nQuadratic cost function typical for thermal generators.\n\n## Technical Details\n\n### Pyomo Model Structure\n\n```python\nConcreteModel()\n  Sets:\n    m.B (buses)\n    m.G (generators)\n    m.L (loads) → aggregated into m.p_d, m.q_d\n    m.S (shunts) → aggregated into m.b_s\n    m.E (branches)\n\n  Variables:\n    m.v[i] ∈ [v_min, v_max]     # Voltage magnitude\n    m.theta[i] ∈ [-π, π]        # Voltage angle\n    m.pg[g] ∈ [p_min, p_max]    # Gen active power\n    m.qg[g] ∈ [q_min, q_max]    # Gen reactive power\n    m.s_from[e], m.s_to[e]      # Branch flows\n\n  Objective:\n    min Σ_g (c₂_g · pg[g]² + c₁_g · pg[g] + c₀_g)\n\n  Constraints:\n    m.balance_p[i]: ∑P_gen - P_load = ∑P_flows\n    m.balance_q[i]: ∑Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = ∑Q_flows\n    m.limit_s_from[e]: s_from[e]² = P² + Q²\n    m.limit_s_to[e]: s_to[e]² = P² + Q²\n    m.enforce_limit[e]: s_from[e] ≤ s_max[e]\n```\n\n### Input Data Format (JSON)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"reference_bus\": 1,\n  \"base_mva\": 100.0,\n\n  \"buses\": {\n    \"1\": {\"type\": \"slack\", \"v_min\": 0.95, \"v_max\": 1.05},\n    \"2\": {\"type\": \"pv\", \"v_min\": 0.95, \"v_max\": 1.05},\n    \"3\": {\"type\": \"pq\", \"v_min\": 0.90, \"v_max\": 1.10}\n  },\n\n  \"generators\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"bus\": 1,\n      \"p_min\": 0,     \"p_max\": 500,    # MW\n      \"q_min\": -200,  \"q_max\": 200,    # MVAr\n      \"c2\": 0.10,     \"c1\": 20.0,      # Cost coefficients\n      \"c0\": 0.0\n    }\n  },\n\n  \"loads\": {\n    \"1\": {\"bus\": 2, \"p_d\": 200, \"q_d\": 40}\n  },\n\n  \"shunts\": {},\n\n  \"branches\": {\n    \"1\": {\n      \"from_bus\": 1,    \"to_bus\": 2,\n      \"r\": 0.01,        \"x\": 0.05,      # Resistance, reactance (pu)\n      \"b\": 0.02,                         # Shunt susceptance (pu)\n      \"s_max\": 500,                      # Thermal limit (MVA)\n      \"tap\": 1.0,       \"phase\": 0.0    # Transformer tap, phase shift (deg)\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Output Format (report.json)\n\n```json\n{\n  \"summary\": {\n    \"total_cost_per_hour\": 123456.78,\n    \"total_load_MW\": 500.0,\n    \"total_load_MVAr\": 100.0,\n    \"total_generation_MW\": 505.0,\n    \"total_generation_MVAr\": 110.0,\n    \"total_losses_MW\": 5.0,\n    \"solver_status\": \"optimal\"\n  },\n  \"generators\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"bus\": 1,\n      \"pg_MW\": 250.5,\n      \"qg_MVAr\": 50.0,\n      \"pmin_MW\": 0.0,\n      \"pmax_MW\": 500.0,\n      \"qmin_MVAr\": -200.0,\n      \"qmax_MVAr\": 200.0\n    }\n  ],\n  \"buses\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"vm_pu\": 1.020,\n      \"va_deg\": 0.0,\n      \"vmin_pu\": 0.95,\n      \"vmax_pu\": 1.05\n    }\n  ],\n  \"most_loaded_branches\": [\n    {\n      \"from_bus\": 1,\n      \"to_bus\": 2,\n      \"loading_pct\": 85.0,\n      \"flow_from_MVA\": 425.0,\n      \"flow_to_MVA\": 420.0,\n      \"limit_MVA\": 500.0\n    }\n  ],\n  \"feasibility_check\": {\n    \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.001,\n    \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.002,\n    \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": 0.0,\n    \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": 0.0\n  }\n}\n```\n\n## Solver Selection Guide\n\n### IPOPT (Recommended)\n- **Install**: `conda install -c conda-forge ipopt`\n- **Type**: Interior point NLP solver\n- **Pros**: Free, robust, fast on AC-OPF\n- **Cons**: Local optimum only\n- **Time**: 30 min for typical utility network\n\n### KNITRO\n- **Type**: Commercial, robust NLP solver\n- **Pros**: Faster, better handling of difficult cases\n- **Cons**: Licensed (expensive), requires activation\n- **Time**: 15 min for typical utility network\n\n### BARON\n- **Type**: Global optimization (MIP/NLP)\n- **Pros**: Global optimum guaranteed\n- **Cons**: Much slower, for small problems\n- **Time**: Hours for large networks\n\n## Use Cases\n\n### 1. Core Solver: `ac_opf_complete.py`\n**Purpose**: Implements the AC OPF problem formulation\n\n**Key Classes**:\n- `ACOPFModel`: Main solver class using Pyomo\n  - `build()`: Constructs optimization model with all equations\n  - `solve()`: Executes solver (IPOPT, KNITRO, etc.)\n  - `extract_solution()`: Returns raw solution data\n\n- `NetworkData`: Data container matching math-model notation\n  - Sets: buses, generators, loads, shunts, branches\n  - Parameters: voltage bounds, power limits, cost coefficients\n\n**Mathematical Implementation**:\n- Variables: Bus voltages (V_i, θ_i), generator dispatch (P_g, Q_g), branch flows\n- Objective: Minimize ∑(c₂·P² + c₁·P + c₀)\n- Constraints:\n  - Power balance (Kirchhoff current law)\n  - AC power flow equations (π-model with tap/phase shifts)\n  - Voltage magnitude limits\n  - Generator active/reactive power limits\n  - Branch apparent power limits\n\n**Solver Interface**: Uses Pyomo's SolverFactory\n- Default: IPOPT (open-source, excellent for AC-OPF)\n- Alternatives: KNITRO, BARON, SNOPT\n\n### 2. Load case file\n  2. Build AC OPF model\n  3. Solve with specified solver\n  4. Extract solution\n  5. Generate report\n  6. Print summary\n\n**Command-line Interface**:\n```bash\nusage: plan_base_case.py [case_file] [--solver SOLVER] [--output FILE] [--verbose]\n```\n\n### 4. USER INPUT (JSON case file)\n   ├─ Network topology (buses, branches)\n   ├─ Generator data (limits, costs)\n   └─ Load data (P, Q demand)\n        ↓\n2. CASE VALIDATION (validate_case.py)\n   ├─ Check structural validity\n   ├─ Verify constraints\n   └─ Warn of potential issues\n        ↓\n3. AC OPF MODEL BUILD (ac_opf_complete.py)\n   ├─ Create Pyomo ConcreteModel\n   ├─ Define variables (V, θ, P_g, Q_g)\n   ├─ Add objective function (cost minimization)\n   ├─ Add constraints:\n   │  ├─ Power balance (Kirchhoff)\n   │  ├─ AC power flow equations\n   │  ├─ Voltage limits\n   │  ├─ Power limits\n   │  └─ Branch limits\n   └─ Pass to solver\n        ↓\n4. SOLUTION EXTRACTION\n   ├─ Generator dispatch (P_g, Q_g)\n   ├─ Bus voltages (V, θ)\n   ├─ Branch flows\n   └─ Feasibility metrics\n        ↓\n6. REPORT GENERATION (report_generator.py)\n   ├─ Format output for JSON\n   ├─ Calculate summary statistics\n   ├─ Check feasibility\n   ├─ Identify congestion\n   └─ Write report.json\n        ↓\n7. Detailed Solution\n\nExtract specific information from report.json:\n\n```python\nimport json\n\nwith open('report.json') as f:\n    report = json.load(f)\n\n# Check if AC-feasible\nis_feasible = all(\n    report['feasibility_check'][metric] < 0.001\n    for metric in ['max_p_mismatch_MW', 'max_voltage_violation_pu']\n)\nprint(f\"AC-Feasible: {is_feasible}\")\n\n# Find most expensive generator\nmost_expensive = max(\n    report['generators'],\n    key=lambda g: g['pg_MW'] * g['c1']\n)\nprint(f\"Most expensive: Gen {most_expensive['id']} at {most_expensive['pg_MW']} MW\")\n\n# Find tightest constraint\ntightest_branch = max(\n    report['most_loaded_branches'],\n    key=lambda b: b['loading_pct']\n)\nprint(f\"Tightest: Bus {tightest_branch['from_bus']}→{tightest_branch['to_bus']} \"\n      f\"at {tightest_branch['loading_pct']:.1f}%\")\n\n# Check total losses\ntotal_loss = report['summary']['total_losses_MW']\ntotal_load = report['summary']['total_load_MW']\nloss_pct = 100.0 * total_loss / total_load\nprint(f\"Losses: {total_loss:.1f} MW ({loss_pct:.2f}%)\")\n```\n\n## Step 6: Action Items for Operations\n\nBased on the report:\n\n1. **If feasible** (✓):\n   - ✓ Base case approved for operations\n   - ✓ Day-ahead schedule is AC feasible\n   - ✓ No preventive actions needed\n\n2. **If infeasible** (✗):\n   - ✗ Review constraint violations\n   - ✗ Consider:\n     - Increased voltage bounds\n     - Locational generation dispatch\n     - Demand reduction\n   - ✗ Re-solve with modifications\n\n3. **If branch overloaded**:\n   - ✗ Identify which branch\n   - ✗ Consider:\n     - Rescheduling generation\n     - Line outage contingencies\n     - Phase shifter settings\n   - ✗ Monitor for real-time operations\n\n## Example: Contingency Analysis\n\nTo check N-1 contingencies:\n\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\n\n# Create outage case by removing branch\nfor branch_id in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do\n    # Copy original case\n    cp peak_hour_case.json outage_case_br${branch_id}.json\n\n    # Remove branch (manual JSON edit or script)\n\n    # Solve\n    python plan_base_case.py outage_case_br${branch_id}.json \\\n      --output \"BaseCase_Outage_Br${branch_id}.json\"\n\n    # Check feasibility\n    feas=$(grep \"is AC-FEASIBLE\" report.json)\n    echo \"Branch $branch_id outage: $feas\"\ndone\n```\n\n## Example: Load Forecast Sensitivity\n\nTest multiple load scenarios:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Load base case\nwith open('peak_hour_case.json') as f:\n    base_case = json.load(f)\n\n# Test different load levels\nfor load_pct in [80, 90, 100, 110, 120]:\n    # Modify loads\n    case = json.loads(json.dumps(base_case))  # Deep copy\n    for load_id, load in case['loads'].items():\n        load['p_d'] *= load_pct / 100.0\n        load['q_d'] *= load_pct / 100.0\n\n    # Save\n    case_file = f\"case_load{load_pct}.json\"\n    with open(case_file, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(case, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Solve\n    report_file = f\"report_load{load_pct}.json\"\n    subprocess.run([\n        'python', 'plan_base_case.py', case_file,\n        '--output', report_file\n    ])\n\n    # Extract status\n    with open(report_file) as f:\n        report = json.load(f)\n\n    status = report['summary']['solver_status']\n    cost = report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']\n    print(f\"Load {load_pct}%: {status}, Cost: ${cost:,.0f}/hr\")\n```\n\nExpected output:\n```\nLoad 80%: optimal, Cost: $987,654/hr\nLoad 90%: optimal, Cost: $1,102,345/hr\nLoad 100%: optimal, Cost: $1,234,567/hr\nLoad 110%: feasible, Cost: $1,456,789/hr\nLoad 120%: infeasible, violations detected\n```\n\n## Real-World Considerations\n\n### Typical Workflow\n```\nEvery Morning:\n  8:00 AM - Receive load forecast for peak hour (2-4 PM)\n  8:15 AM - Create peak_hour_case.json with forecast\n  8:20 AM - Run: python plan_base_case.py peak_hour_case.json\n  8:25 AM - Review report.json\n  8:30 AM - Approve or modify market schedule\n  9:00 AM - Publish base case to traders/operations\n```\n\n### Common Modifications\n- **Load adjustment**: Use forecast uncertainty\n- **Generator commitment**: Based on cost and reserve requirements\n- **Line outages**: Include json.loads(json.dumps(base_case))  # Deep copy\n    for load_id, load in case['loads'].items():\n        load['p_d'] *= load_pct / 100.0\n        load['q_d'] *= load_pct / 100.0\n\n    # Save\n    case_file = f\"case_load{load_pct}.json\"\n    with open(case_file, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(case, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Solve\n    report_file = f\"report_load{load_pct}.json\"\n    subprocess.run([\n        'python', 'plan_base_case.py', case_file,\n        '--output', report_file\n    ])\n\n    # Extract status\n    with open(report_file) as f:\n        report = json.load(f)\n\n    status = report['summary']['solver_status']\n    cost = report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']\n    print(f\"Load {load_pct}%: {status}, Cost: ${cost:,.0f}/hr\")\n```\n\nExpected output:\n```\nLoad 80%: optimal, Cost: $987,654/hr\nLoad 90%: optimal, Cost: $1,102,345/hr\nLoad 100%: optimal, Cost: $1,234,567/hr\nLoad 110%: feasible, Cost: $1,456,789/hr\nLoad 120%: infeasible, violations detected\n```\n\n## Real-World Considerations\n\n### Typical Workflow\n```\nEvery Morning:\n  8:00 AM - Receive load forecast for peak hour (2-4 PM)\n  8:15 AM - Create peak_hour_case.json with forecast\n  8:20 AM - Run: python plan_base_case.py peak_hour_case.json\n  8:25 AM - Review report.json\n  8:30 AM - Approve or modify market schedule\n  9:00 AM - Publish base case to traders/operations\n```\n\n### Common Modifications\n- **Load adjustment**: Use forecast uncertainty\n- **Generator commitment**: Based on cost and reserve requirements\n- **Line outages**: Include planned maintenance\n- **Special equipment**: Phase shifters, FACTS devices\n\n### Performance Targets\n- **Solve time**: < 1 minute for typical utility\n- **Convergence**: 99% success with good {branch['from_bus']}→{branch['to_bus']}: \"\n              f\"{branch['loading_pct']:.1f}% loaded\")\n```\n\n### Problem: Voltage violations\n\nCheck and address:\n```python\nimport json\nwith Constraints)\n    │  ├─ Active Power Balance: P_gen - P_load = Σ P_ij\n    │  └─ Reactive Power Balance: Q_gen - Q_load = Σ Q_ij\n    │\n    ├─ Component Limits\n    │  ├─ Generator Active: p_min ≤ P_g ≤ p_max\n    │  ├─ Generator Reactive: q_min ≤ Q_g ≤ q_max\n    │  ├─ Voltage Magnitude: v_min ≤ |V| ≤ v_max\n    │  └─ Branch Apparent Power: |S_ij| ≤ s_max\n    │\n    ├─ Network Physics (AC Equations)\n    │  ├─ Power Flow from i→j:\n    │  │  P_ij = |V_i|²·G/τ² - |V_i||V_j|(G·cos(θ_ij) + B·sin(θ_ij))/τ\n    │  │  Q_ij = -|V_i|²·B/τ² - |V_i||V_j|(G·sin(θ_ij) - B·cos(θ_ij))/τ\n    │  │\n    │  └─ Power Flow to j←i:\n    │     P_ji = |V_j|²·G - |V_i||V_j|(G·cos(θ_ij) - B·sin(θ_ij))/τ\n    │     Q_ji = -|V_j|²·B - |V_i||V_j|(G·sin(θ_ij) + B·cos(θ_ij))/τ\n    │\n    └─ System Constraints\n       └─ Reference Bus Angle: θ_ref = 0\n\n\nSOLVER INTEGRATION\n==================\n\n    Pyomo Model (ConcreteModel)\n    │\n    ├─ Sets\n    │  ├─ m.B (buses)\n    │  ├─ m.G BARON (global optimization)\n    └─ Others (SNOPT, etc.)\n    \n              │\n              ▼\n    \n    Solution Object\n    ├─ Solver status (optimal, feasible, error)\n    ├─ Termination condition\n    ├─ Variable values\n    └─ Objective value\n\n\nFEASIBILITY CHECK PIPELINE\n==========================\n\n    Solution from Solver\n           │\n           ├─ Power Balance Check\n           │  ├─ Calculate P_mismatch = |P_gen - P_load - Σ P_ij|\n           │  ├─ Calculate Q_mismatch = |Q_gen - Q_load - Σ Q_ij|\n           │  └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.01)\n           │\n           ├─ Voltage Limit Check\n           │  ├─ For each bus:\n           │  │  ├─ If |V| < V_min: violation = V_min - |V|\n           │  │  ├─ If |V| > V_max: violation = |V| - V_max\n           │  │  └─ Track maximum\n           │  └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.001 pu)\n           │\n           └─ Branch Limit Check\n              ├─ For each branch:\n              │  ├─ Calculate |S_ij| = √(P² + Q²)\n              │  ├─ If |S_ij| > S_max: overload = |S_ij| - S_max\n              │  └─ Track maximum\n              └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.01 MVA)\n\n              │\n              ▼\n    \n    Feasibility Report\n    ├─ max_p_mismatch_MW\n    ├─ max_q_mismatch_MVAr\n    ├─ max_voltage_violation_pu\n    └─ max_branch_overload_MVA\n    \n    If all ≈ 0: ✓ AC-FEASIBLE\n    If any > threshold: ✗ INFEASIBLE\n\n\nREPORT JSON STRUCTURE\n=====================\n\n    report.json\n    │\n    ├─ summary\n    │  ├─ total_cost_per_hour (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_load_MW (numeric)\n    │ validate_feasibility()\n    │  └─ print_report()\n    │\n    └─ validate_file()\n    └─ main()\n\n\nCONSTRAINT TREE\n===============\n\n    Power Flow Feasibility\n    │\n    ├─ Nodal Balance (Bus Constraints)\n    │  ├─ Active Power Balance: P_gen - P_load = Σ P_ij\n    │  └─ Reactive Power Balance: Q_gen - Q_load = Σ Q_ij\n    │\n    ├─ Component Limits\n    │  ├─ Generator Active: p_min ≤ P_g ≤ p_max\n    │  ├─ Generator Reactive: q_min ≤ Q_g ≤ q_max\n    │  ├─ Voltage Magnitude: v_min ≤ |V| ≤ v_max\n    │  └─ Branch Apparent Power: |S_ij| ≤ s_max\n    │\n    ├─ Network Physics (AC Equations)\n    │  ├─ Power Flow from i→j:\n    │  │  P_ij = |V_i|²·G/τ² - |V_i||V_j|(G·cos(θ_ij) + B·sin(θ_ij))/τ\n    │  │  Q_ij = -|V_i|²·B/τ² - |V_i||V_j|(G·sin(θ_ij) - B·cos(θ_ij))/τ\n    │  │\n    │  └─ Power Flow to j←i:\n    │     P_ji = |V_j|²·G - |V_i||V_j|(G·cos(θ_ij) - B·sin(θ_ij))/τ\n    │     Q_ji = -|V_j|²·B - |V_i||V_j|(G·sin(θ_ij) + B·cos(θ_ij))/τ\n    │\n    └─ System Constraints\n       └─ Reference Bus Angle: θ_ref = 0\n\n\nSOLVER INTEGRATION\n==================\n\n    Pyomo Model (ConcreteModel)\n    │\n    ├─ Sets\n    │  ├─ m.B (buses)\n    │  ├─ m.G └─ m.qg (continuous, bounds)\n    │\n    ├─ Objective\n    │  └─ m.cost (Objective rule)\n    │\n    └─ Constraints\n       ├─ m.balance_p (rule-based)\n       ├─ m.balance_q (rule-based)\n       ├─ m.limit_s_from (rule-based)\n       └─ ...\n\n              │\n              ▼\n    \n    SolverFactory(solver_name)\n    │\n    ├─ IPOPT (NLP interior point)\n    ├─ KNITRO (NLP commercial)\n    ├─ BARON (global optimization)\n    └─ Others (SNOPT, etc.)\n    \n              │\n              ▼\n    \n    Solution Object\n    ├─ Solver status (optimal, feasible, error)\n    ├─ Termination condition\n    ├─ Variable values\n    └─ Objective value\n\n\nFEASIBILITY CHECK PIPELINE\n==========================\n\n    Solution from Solver\n           │\n           ├─ Power Balance Check\n           │  ├─ Calculate P_mismatch = |P_gen - P_load - Σ P_ij|\n           │  ├─ Calculate Q_mismatch = |Q_gen - Q_load - Σ Q_ij|\n           │  └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.01)\n           │\n Track maximum\n           │  └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.001 pu)\n           │\n           └─ Branch Limit Check\n              ├─ For each branch:\n              │  ├─ Calculate |S_ij| = √(P² + Q²)\n              │  ├─ If |S_ij| > S_max: overload = |S_ij| - S_max\n              │  └─ Track maximum\n              └─ Compare to tolerance (< 0.01 MVA)\n\n              │\n              ▼\n    \n    Feasibility Report\n    ├─ max_p_mismatch_MW\n    ├─ max_q_mismatch_MVAr\n    ├─ max_voltage_violation_pu\n    └─ max_branch_overload_MVA\n    \n    If all ≈ 0: ✓ AC-FEASIBLE\n    If any > threshold: ✗ INFEASIBLE\n\n\nREPORT JSON STRUCTURE\n=====================\n\n    report.json\n    │\n    ├─ summary\n    │  ├─ total_cost_per_hour (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_load_MW (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_load_MVAr (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_generation_MW (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_generation_MVAr (numeric)\n    │  ├─ total_losses_MW (numeric)\n    │  └─ solver_status (string)\n    │\n    ├─ limits: v_min ≤ |V| ≤ v_max\n- ✅ Generator limits: p_min ≤ P ≤ p_max, q_min ≤ Q ≤ q_max\n- ✅ Branch limits: |S_ij| ≤ S_max\n- ✅ Transformer modeling (tap, phase shift)\n\n## Performance
